# March 1993 PDF restoration

## Current outcome

Updated 2026-11-03. The full March 1993 restoration goal is active under
PLAN-PDF.md and PLAN-OCR.md. All 52 canonical identities are initialized:
forty-one articles are readable/sample-reviewed, no article is partial, seven headings
are non-owning groups, three children are final section references and one identity
remains unresolved. All 433 physical source pages and all 25 original exclusions across 23 mixed pages
are source-accounted. Final0037 eligibility/disposition, cover, standalone collection
and issue closeout remain pending. Canonical titles,
bylines, order, parents and contents references remain unchanged. No global
PDF/folio offset is inferred.
